- **cs-weekly-review**: Walks a user through a complete GTD weekly review — GET CLEAR (collect, process inboxes to zero, empty your head), GET CURRENT (next actions, previous + upcoming calendar, waiting-for, project lists), GET CREATIVE (someday/maybe, new ideas) — using deterministic scripts to inventory open loops, gate the checklist, and score commitment health.
